        ore than any other flying saucer contactee, the late George Adamski provoked controversy, Supposedly the Pope granted him an audience, and

 M      he once had lunch with the Queen of the Netherlands. In 1952, In the California desert, a scout ship landed and a being from another planet
        emerged from the craft In full view of six witnesses. Adamski approached the golden-haired allen and conversed with him In sign language for
a period of several minutes.
  Adamski's first book FLYING S AUCERS HAVE LANDED was a run away bestseller both In the United States and overseas. In this book he told about
his many attempts to photograph space craft- which he supposedly did on several occasions, producing the clearest shots of UFO's ever published
anywhere. He also wrote of subsequent contacts with this same being and what the program of the Space Brothers was to Include. Later, In his book,
INSIDE THE SPACESHIPS he told about traveling out Into space and seeing the "firefly phenomena" which our own astronauts later reported.
  Now, after many years, the texts of these two books (minus the original historical reference section by Desmond Leslie) have been put together In one
deluxe, quality soft covered edition of 300 pages. For those who missed reading these books by George Adamski, because they have only recently
become Interested In the field, or due to the fact that their copies of the original editions have been read by dozens of friends, now you can get this.

        might be considered to be UFO by untrained observers. The crew Insisted
